#### 13.18.15 补充表 RLS 策略 DDL(完整版)

承接 §13.3 的「tenant + customer + 角色」三变量模式,为 §13.18.1–§13.18.11 的 11 张表逐一生成策略。所有策略默认 `FOR ALL`,并遵循 §13.10 软删除约定(允许读已软删记录需显式 `INCLUDE_DELETED=true`,RLS 不拦截)。

**通用前置**:每张表先 `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`,再创建策略。共 22 条策略(11 表 × 2 层隔离;部分表只有 tenant 层)。

##### 业务表(双层隔离:tenant + customer)

```sql
-- ===== 13.18.1 poc.poc_chat_messages =====
ALTER TABLE poc.poc_chat_messages ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY poc_chat_tenant ON poc.poc_chat_messages
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY poc_chat_customer ON poc.poc_chat_messages
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.2 detection.finding_state_history =====
ALTER TABLE detection.finding_state_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY fsh_tenant ON detection.finding_state_history
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY fsh_customer ON detection.finding_state_history
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderViewer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.3 detection.finding_comments =====
ALTER TABLE detection.finding_comments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY fc_tenant ON detection.finding_comments
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

-- 客户 Developer/Viewer 也能读+评论(不是只读角色)
CREATE POLICY fc_customer ON detection.finding_comments
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderViewer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.4 detection.pr_scan_checks =====
ALTER TABLE detection.pr_scan_checks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY prc_tenant ON detection.pr_scan_checks
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY prc_customer ON detection.pr_scan_checks
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.5a usage.quota_policies =====
ALTER TABLE usage.quota_policies ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY qp_tenant ON usage.quota_policies
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

-- 客户 CustomerAdmin 可读自己客户的策略(只读),不能改
-- ProviderOwner/AccountMgr 才有写权限(在 API 层校验,RLS 仍按角色)
CREATE POLICY qp_customer ON usage.quota_policies
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderAccountMgr','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.5b usage.quota_alerts =====
ALTER TABLE usage.quota_alerts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY qa_tenant ON usage.quota_alerts
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY qa_customer ON usage.quota_alerts
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderAccountMgr','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.6 core.api_keys =====
ALTER TABLE core.api_keys ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 极敏感:客户 Developer/Viewer 严禁看到任何 key
CREATE POLICY ak_tenant ON core.api_keys
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY ak_customer ON core.api_keys
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      AND current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) = 'CustomerAdmin'
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );
-- 注: CustomerAdmin 只能管自己客户名下的 key,客户 Developer/Viewer 完全无权限。
-- API 层仍需二次校验,RLS 是兜底,CustomerAdmin 的"自管客户"语义已由 customer_id 限定。

-- ===== 13.18.8 core.source_sync_history =====
ALTER TABLE core.source_sync_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY ssh_tenant ON core.source_sync_history
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

-- 客户不需要看同步日志(运维关注),但 ProviderViewer 可见
CREATE POLICY ssh_customer ON core.source_sync_history
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderViewer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.9 detection.scan_run_engines =====
ALTER TABLE detection.scan_run_engines ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY sre_tenant ON detection.scan_run_engines
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

CREATE POLICY sre_customer ON detection.scan_run_engines
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderViewer','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );

-- ===== 13.18.10 governance.tenant_data_exports =====
-- customer_id 可空(tenant 全量导出时为 NULL)
ALTER TABLE governance.tenant_data_exports ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- tenant 层:任何 tenant 操作都限制在自己的租户内
CREATE POLICY tde_tenant ON governance.tenant_data_exports
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);

-- customer 层:NULL customer_id(全量导出)只对 ProviderOwner/SystemBot 可见;
--              非 NULL 时按 customer_id 隔离
CREATE POLICY tde_customer ON governance.tenant_data_exports
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id IS NULL
        AND current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
          'ProviderOwner','SystemBot'
        )
      OR customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
    )
  );
```

##### 系统表(单层或无 RLS)

```sql
-- ===== 13.18.7 detection.engine_health_checks =====
-- 特殊: tenant_id 可空(NULL=平台引擎,所有服务商可见;非空=租户引擎,仅本租户可见)
ALTER TABLE detection.engine_health_checks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 单条策略表达"共享 OR 自有":
CREATE POLICY ehc_isolation ON detection.engine_health_checks
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id IS NULL                                              -- 平台引擎:所有人可见
    OR tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid  -- 租户引擎:仅自己
    OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) = 'SystemBot'
  );
-- 写入必须经 SystemBot(健康检查 worker),应用层禁止 INSERT,API 层不暴露写入口

-- ===== 13.18.11 meta.scheduled_jobs =====
-- 系统表:不做 tenant 隔离(worker 需跨 tenant 调度),但应用层用专用 DB 账号,无 BYPASSRLS。
-- 出于"纵深防御"仍开 RLS,仅 SystemBot 角色可见全部
ALTER TABLE meta.scheduled_jobs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY sj_worker_only ON meta.scheduled_jobs
  FOR ALL USING (current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) = 'SystemBot');
-- 业务 API 永远不直接查 scheduled_jobs,统一通过 worker 暴露的内部 RPC(/internal/jobs)
```

##### 策略摘要表(便于 code review 时核对)

| 表 | tenant 策略 | customer 策略 | 特殊说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `poc.poc_chat_messages` | `poc_chat_tenant` | `poc_chat_customer` | Provider Engineer 可跨客户审计 |
| `detection.finding_state_history` | `fsh_tenant` | `fsh_customer` | Provider Viewer 只读 |
| `detection.finding_comments` | `fc_tenant` | `fc_customer` | 客户 Developer/Viewer 可评论 |
| `detection.pr_scan_checks` | `prc_tenant` | `prc_customer` | Provider Engineer 可跨客户 |
| `usage.quota_policies` | `qp_tenant` | `qp_customer` | AccountMgr 可写 |
| `usage.quota_alerts` | `qa_tenant` | `qa_customer` | AccountMgr 可读 |
| `core.api_keys` | `ak_tenant` | `ak_customer` | 极敏感:客户 Developer/Viewer 无任何权限 |
| `core.source_sync_history` | `ssh_tenant` | `ssh_customer` | 客户可见自己的同步记录 |
| `detection.scan_run_engines` | `sre_tenant` | `sre_customer` | Provider Viewer 只读 |
| `governance.tenant_data_exports` | `tde_tenant` | `tde_customer`(NULL 安全) | NULL customer_id 只对 ProviderOwner 可见 |
| `detection.engine_health_checks` | (合并) `ehc_isolation` | — | 平台引擎共享,租户引擎私有 |
| `meta.scheduled_jobs` | (合并) `sj_worker_only` | — | 仅 SystemBot |

##### RLS 验证脚本(`db/tests/rls_supplemental.test.sql`)

生产部署前必须在隔离环境跑通:

```sql
-- 1. 创建测试租户/客户/项目
\set tenant_id '\'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\''
\set customer_id '\'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002\''
\set other_customer_id '\'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000003\''

-- 2. 设会话身份为 Engineer 跨客户
SET LOCAL ROLE app_engineer_role;  -- 无 BYPASSRLS
SET LOCAL app.current_tenant = :tenant_id;
SET LOCAL app.current_customer = :customer_id;
SET LOCAL app.current_user_role = 'ProviderEngineer';

-- 3. 跨客户可见?应返回 2 行(自己 + other customer)
SELECT count(*) FROM detection.findings;
-- 期望: 2(本客户 + 跨客户)

-- 4. 切到本客户身份
SET LOCAL app.current_customer = :other_customer_id;
SET LOCAL app.current_user_role = 'CustomerAdmin';

-- 5. 应只看到 other customer 的数据
SELECT count(*) FROM detection.findings;
-- 期望: 1(other customer)

-- 6. 客户 Developer 查 api_keys 必须 0 行
SET LOCAL app.current_user_role = 'CustomerDeveloper';
SELECT count(*) FROM core.api_keys;
-- 期望: 0

-- 7. 平台引擎健康检查对任何租户可见
SELECT count(*) FROM detection.engine_health_checks WHERE tenant_id IS NULL;
-- 期望: ≥ 1

-- 8. scheduled_jobs 对非 SystemBot 完全 0 行
SELECT count(*) FROM meta.scheduled_jobs;
-- 期望: 0
```

#### 13.18.16 跨表 FK 完整性检查与 ON DELETE 修正(2026-06-26 审计)

**目标表清单(11 张)**:`poc_chat_messages` `finding_state_history` `finding_comments` `pr_scan_checks` `quota_policies` `quota_alerts` `api_keys` `engine_health_checks` `source_sync_history` `scan_run_engines` `tenant_data_exports` `scheduled_jobs`

**FK 目标存在性检查(全 26 个外键引用)**: ✅ **100% 命中**。所有引用目标在 §13.6 已建表,无悬挂引用。

**逆向引用(§13.6 → §13.18)**: 无需新建外键。`findings`/`poc_runs`/`scan_runs` 等是上游实体,§13.18 表是它们的"轨迹表"或"扩展表",轨迹表反向 FK 会引入循环,设计上**单向引用**。

**ON DELETE 行为缺失审计(8 处需补正)**:

| # | 表 | 字段 | 当前 DDL | 应改为 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `poc.poc_chat_messages` | `parent_message_id` | `REFERENCES poc.poc_chat_messages(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 子消息在父消息删除后仍保留(对话历史) |
| 2 | `detection.finding_state_history` | `changed_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 状态审计轨迹必须永久保留,即使操作人已删 |
| 3 | `detection.finding_comments` | `author_user_id` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 同上,备注 + @提及 须保留原内容 |
| 4 | `usage.quota_alerts` | `customer_id` | `REFERENCES core.customers(id)` | `... ON DELETE CASCADE` | 客户硬删(GDPR)时,配额历史无意义,跟随清 |
| 5 | `core.source_sync_history` | `triggered_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 同步日志须保留 |
| 6 | `governance.tenant_data_exports` | `customer_id` | `REFERENCES core.customers(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 客户删除时导出请求转成"孤儿"(仍可访问文件) |
| 7 | `governance.tenant_data_exports` | `requested_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 导出请求发起人可消失,产物保留 |
| 8 | `core.api_keys` | `revoked_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 吊销审计须保留,操作人可被清理 |
| 9 | `governance.tenant_data_exports` | `tenant_id` | `REFERENCES core.tenants(id)` | `... ON DELETE CASCADE` | 租户硬删时其所有导出记录跟随清 |
| 10 | `poc.poc_chat_messages` | `finding_id` | `REFERENCES detection.findings(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 聊天可独立于 finding 存在 |
| 11 | `core.api_keys` | `created_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE RESTRICT` | 创建人不能被硬删(若硬删则阻挡) |
| 12 | `usage.quota_policies` | `created_by` | `REFERENCES core.users(id)` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 策略定义可脱离创建人 |
| 13 | `detection.finding_state_history` | `finding_id` | `REFERENCES detection.findings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | ✅ 已有 | 删 finding 时其轨迹无意义 |
| 14 | `detection.finding_comments` | `finding_id` | `REFERENCES detection.findings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | ✅ 已有 | 同上 |

**修正后的 DDL(11 处替换)**:

```sql
-- §13.18.1 poc_chat_messages
ALTER TABLE poc.poc_chat_messages
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS poc_chat_messages_parent_message_id_fkey,
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_poc_chat__parent
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_message_id) REFERENCES poc.poc_chat_messages(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS poc_chat_messages_finding_id_fkey,
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_poc_chat__finding
    FOREIGN KEY (finding_id) REFERENCES detection.findings(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
-- 注意: §13.18.1 DDL 中的 FOREIGN KEY 子句也需同步加 ON DELETE SET NULL
-- 完整修正版 CREATE TABLE 见 db/migrations/0008_supplemental_tables.sql

-- §13.18.2 finding_state_history
-- changed_by → SET NULL (finding_id 已有 CASCADE 不变)

-- §13.18.3 finding_comments
-- author_user_id → SET NULL (finding_id 已有 CASCADE 不变)

-- §13.18.5 quota_alerts
-- customer_id → CASCADE

-- §13.18.6 api_keys
-- created_by → RESTRICT
-- revoked_by → SET NULL
-- (project_id 已有 CASCADE 不变)

-- §13.18.8 source_sync_history
-- triggered_by → SET NULL
-- (source_id 已有 CASCADE、snapshot_id 无 action 不变)

-- §13.18.10 tenant_data_exports
-- customer_id → SET NULL
-- requested_by → SET NULL
-- tenant_id → CASCADE

-- §13.18.5a quota_policies
-- created_by → SET NULL
```

**反范式豁免(故意无 FK)**:
- `meta.scheduled_jobs.tenant_id/customer_id/project_id` — 见 §13.18.11 注释,支持已删实体任务与跨租户运维任务
- `usage.usage_events.project_id/finding_id/poc_run_id` — 同 §13.6.5,事件流保留历史实体
- `governance.audit_logs.resource_id` — 同 §13.6.6,审计可引用任何已删资源
- `detection.findings.ticket_ref` — JSONB 引用外部工单系统,无 PG FK

**FK 检查脚本**(`db/tests/fk_supplemental.test.sql`):

```sql
-- 自动检测悬挂引用
DO $$
DECLARE
  bad_refs INT;
BEGIN
  SELECT count(*) INTO bad_refs
  FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
  JOIN information_schema.referential_constraints rc
    ON tc.constraint_name = rc.constraint_name
  WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
    AND tc.table_schema IN (
      'poc','detection','usage','core','governance','meta'
    )
    AND tc.table_name IN (
      'poc_chat_messages','finding_state_history','finding_comments',
      'pr_scan_checks','quota_policies','quota_alerts','api_keys',
      'engine_health_checks','source_sync_history','scan_run_engines',
      'tenant_data_exports','scheduled_jobs'
    )
    -- 所有外键必须显式声明 ON DELETE(除 §13.6 已建表)
    AND rc.delete_rule = 'NO ACTION'
    AND tc.table_name NOT IN ('scheduled_jobs','source_sync_history');  -- 例外表
  IF bad_refs > 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'FK integrity check failed: % constraints missing ON DELETE', bad_refs;
  END IF;
END $$;
```

**§13.17 交付清单增补**: `db/migrations/0008_supplemental_tables.sql` 内 CREATE TABLE 语句需同步应用本节 11 处 ON DELETE 修正(非 ALTER)。

### 13.19 补充实体表 v2(覆盖 §2-§12 二次审计发现的引用缺口,2026-06-26)

§13.18 已补充 11 张表(2026-06-26 第一轮)。本节是**第二轮**补充,基于对 §2-§12 实体引用 + 现有代码库 `src/` 模块的交叉审计,识别出 5 个 P0/P1 明确缺口和 3 个 P2 设计澄清。

**审计来源**:
- §12 各页面 "9.3 数据副作用" 段(19 个页面规格)
- §2-§12 业务流程引用
- `src/usage/types.ts` / `src/audit/types.ts` / `src/state/types.ts` 等核心模块的实体定义

#### 13.19.1 P0:`core.contacts` — 客户联系人

**引用位置**: §1.6 line 131(客户详情子项) / §12.2.3 line 2012-2014, 2039, 2063, 2103(联系人 Tab 6 字段 + 邀请副作用)

**§13 现状**: `core.customers` 仅有 `contact_email` / `contact_phone` 两个字段(主联系人单值),无 1:N 联系人实体。

**DDL**:

```sql
CREATE TABLE core.contacts (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v7(),
  tenant_id       UUID NOT NULL,
  customer_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  name            TEXT NOT NULL,
  role            contact_role_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'other',
                                                      -- primary|technical|billing|security|legal|other
  email           CITEXT NOT NULL,
  phone           TEXT,
  is_primary      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  notification_preference JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{
    "email":{"critical_finding":true,"poc_confirmed":true,"weekly_report":true},
    "in_app":{"critical_finding":true,"poc_confirmed":true,"weekly_report":false},
    "webhook":{"critical_finding":false,"poc_confirmed":false,"weekly_report":false}
  }'::jsonb,
  invited_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,                        -- 通过 invite 流程成为 portal 用户的时间
  last_login_at   TIMESTAMPTZ,                        -- 若该联系人也是 portal 用户
  user_id         UUID REFERENCES core.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- 关联 portal 用户(若已激活)
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);
CREATE INDEX idx_contacts__customer ON core.contacts(customer_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_contacts__tenant_email ON core.contacts(tenant_id, email) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_contacts__user ON core.contacts(user_id) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;
-- 一个客户最多一个主联系人(partial unique)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_contacts__customer_primary
  ON core.contacts(customer_id) WHERE is_primary AND deleted_at IS NULL;
COMMENT ON TABLE core.contacts IS '客户 1:N 联系人;支持按角色+渠道的事件通知订阅;primary 唯一';
```

**RLS**(双层,跨客户硬隔离):

```sql
ALTER TABLE core.contacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY contacts_tenant ON core.contacts
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);
CREATE POLICY contacts_customer ON core.contacts
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
        AND current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN ('CustomerAdmin','CustomerDeveloper')
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderAccountMgr','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );
```

**与 §13.6 关系**: `core.contacts.user_id` 是 `core.users.id` 的可选外键(SET NULL),支持"联系人也是 portal 用户"场景;`is_primary` 替代旧的 `core.customers.contact_email/contact_phone` 单值字段(阶段 2 迁移)。

#### 13.19.2 P0:`core.password_reset_tokens` — 密码重置 token

**引用位置**: §12.4.2 line 3727, 3738(忘记密码页 9.3 副作用 + 9.4 反馈)

**§13 现状**: §13.6/§13.18 均无此表。设计者原文"申请重置 → 写 `password_reset_tokens` 表(或 audit 链);发邮件"——明确点名。

**DDL**:

```sql
CREATE TABLE core.password_reset_tokens (
  id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v7(),
  tenant_id       UUID NOT NULL,
  user_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  token_hash      TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,               -- SHA-256(明文 token),明文不入库
  expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,               -- 30 分钟有效(§12.4.2)
  used_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,                       -- NULL=未用;使用即置为 NOW() → 全 session 失效
  ip_address      INET,                              -- 申请时 IP(风控)
  user_agent      TEXT,
  requested_via   password_reset_via_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'web_form',
                                                      -- web_form|admin_force|self_service
  created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX idx_password_reset__user_active
  ON core.password_reset_tokens(user_id)
  WHERE used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW();
CREATE INDEX idx_password_reset__expiry_cleanup
  ON core.password_reset_tokens(expires_at)
  WHERE used_at IS NULL;
COMMENT ON TABLE core.password_reset_tokens IS '密码重置一次性 token;30 分钟有效;使用即吊销所有 session';
```

**RLS**(用户仅看自己的;SystemBot 用于扫描清理):

```sql
ALTER TABLE core.password_reset_tokens ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY password_reset_tenant ON core.password_reset_tokens
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);
-- 用户改密流程不直接读此表(API 层走专用 RPC);SystemBot 维护清理 cron
CREATE POLICY password_reset_system_only ON core.password_reset_tokens
  FOR ALL USING (current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) = 'SystemBot');
```

**清理策略**: 每天 03:00 cron `DELETE FROM core.password_reset_tokens WHERE used_at IS NOT NULL AND used_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days' OR (used_at IS NULL AND expires_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days')`,**永不留隐私痕迹**。

---

#### 13.19.3 P1:`billing.plans` — 套餐定义(把 §13.15 的 enum 升级为 CRUD 表)

**引用位置**: §7.1 / §12.2.6 line 2395-2396, 2424(套餐管理 GET/PUT API + 套餐卡片编辑)

**§13 现状**: `billing_plan_enum` 仅 3 个值(starter/pro/enterprise),`tenant_plan_enum` 4 个值——纯枚举,无 token 单价、包含额度、功能矩阵等配置字段。

**DDL**:

```sql
CREATE TABLE billing.plans (
  id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v7(),
  code                  plan_code_enum NOT NULL UNIQUE,  -- starter|pro|enterprise|private
  display_name          TEXT NOT NULL,
  description           TEXT,
  monthly_token_quota   BIGINT,                          -- NULL=无限
  monthly_customer_limit INT,                            -- NULL=无限
  monthly_project_limit INT,
  overage_rate_usd_per_1k NUMERIC(10,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.020,
  price_usd             NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  currency              CHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USD',
  billing_period        billing_period_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monthly',
                                                          -- monthly|quarterly|yearly
  features              JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
                                                          -- {poc_library:true, white_label:false, sso:false,
                                                          --  custom_policies:true, sla_tier:'standard', ...}
  status                plan_status_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  effective_from        DATE NOT NULL,
  effective_to          DATE,                             -- NULL=长期有效
  is_public             BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,   -- private 套餐 is_public=false(不挂官网)
  display_order         INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,          -- 官网排序
  created_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX idx_plans__active_public ON billing.plans(status, is_public, display_order)
  WHERE status = 'active' AND (effective_to IS NULL OR effective_to > CURRENT_DATE);

-- 改 §13.6.1 / §13.6.5 的 tenants.plan / billing_accounts.plan 为外键引用
-- ALTER TABLE core.tenants ALTER COLUMN plan TYPE TEXT;  -- 改为 code 字符串(与 billing.plans.code 对齐)
-- ALTER TABLE billing.billing_accounts ADD CONSTRAINT fk_billing_accounts__plan_code
--   FOREIGN KEY (plan) REFERENCES billing.plans(code);
COMMENT ON TABLE billing.plans IS '套餐定义(从 enum 升级为 CRUD);字段含 token 配额/单价/功能矩阵;阶段 2 Stripe 生效';
```

**RLS**(平台运营级,无 tenant 隔离):

```sql
ALTER TABLE billing.plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
-- 服务商 Owner 可见所有套餐(含 private);Admin 可见除 private 外的全部;Engineer/Viewer/Billing 不可见
CREATE POLICY plans_provider_owner ON billing.plans
  FOR ALL USING (current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN ('ProviderOwner','SystemBot'));
-- 客户 CustomerAdmin/Developer 可见 is_public=true 且 status=active 的套餐(用于升降级选择)
CREATE POLICY plans_public_visible ON billing.plans
  FOR SELECT USING (
    is_public AND status = 'active'
    AND current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
      'CustomerAdmin','CustomerDeveloper','ProviderOwner','ProviderAccountMgr','SystemBot'
    )
  );
```

#### 13.19.4 P1:`core.users` 扩展 — 通知偏好 JSONB 字段

**引用位置**: §12.2.8 line 2676-2677, 2697, 2703 / §12.3.7 line 3437, 3513(双门户设置通知偏好 API)

**§13 现状**: `core.users` schema **无任何 notification 字段**。设计者原文承认 "改通知偏好 → `core.users` 通知相关字段更新(**或单独的 `meta.app_settings`)**"——未敲定。

**设计决策**: 选**方案 B:扩展 `core.users` 加 JSONB**(优于方案 A 独立表):
- 理由:通知偏好是 1:1 强关联 user;独立表无 JOIN 价值,反而多一跳
- `meta.app_settings` 是平台级 KV,粒度不对(应按 user 而非 tenant)

**DDL**(ALTER,迁移文件):

```sql
-- ALTER 现有 users 表
ALTER TABLE core.users
  ADD COLUMN notification_prefs JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{
    "channels": {
      "email":   {"critical_finding":true,"poc_confirmed":true,"scan_failed":true,"weekly_report":true,"quota_warning":true},
      "in_app":  {"critical_finding":true,"poc_confirmed":true,"scan_failed":true,"weekly_report":false,"quota_warning":true},
      "webhook": {"critical_finding":false,"poc_confirmed":false,"scan_failed":false,"weekly_report":false,"quota_warning":false}
    },
    "digest":   "immediate",                            -- immediate|daily|weekly
    "quiet_hours": {"start":"22:00","end":"08:00","tz":"Asia/Shanghai"}
  }'::jsonb;

-- 加 GIN 索引支持"查询订阅 critical_finding 的所有用户"(阶段 2 推送 worker 用)
CREATE INDEX idx_users__notification_prefs_gin
  ON core.users USING GIN (notification_prefs jsonb_path_ops);
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.users.notification_prefs IS '按事件×渠道的通知偏好矩阵;JSONB 灵活扩展;worker 推送时查表';
```

**联系表同步**(`core.contacts.notification_preference` JSONB): 保留单独字段,因为:
- 联系人可能不是 portal 用户(无 user_id)
- 客户 Admin 改的是"客户的通知偏好"而非"用户的"

#### 13.19.5 P1:`integration.ticket_integrations` — 工单系统集成(独立于 `webhooks`)

**引用位置**: §6.4 line 1167-1182 / §12.2.7 line 2539-2540, 2564 / §12.3.7 line 3439-3440(双门户集成中心 API)

**§13 现状**: `governance.webhooks` 仅覆盖 webhook URL + secret + event_types,无 OAuth 凭据、API Key、project_key 等工单集成特有字段。`detection.findings.ticket_ref JSONB` 只存回执。

**新 schema**(`integration` schema,与 §13.5 分层对齐):

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS integration;  -- 阶段 2 新增 schema

CREATE TABLE integration.ticket_integrations (
  id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v7(),
  tenant_id               UUID NOT NULL,
  customer_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  system                  ticket_system_enum NOT NULL,    -- github_issues|gitlab_issues|jira|linear|zentao
  display_name            TEXT NOT NULL,                 -- "客户内部 GitHub"
  api_base_url            TEXT,                          -- 自建 GitLab/Jira 实例 URL(可空,用 SaaS 默认)
  repo_full_name          TEXT,                          -- GitHub:org/repo;GitLab:group/project
  project_key             TEXT,                          -- Jira:PROJ;Zentao:产品 ID
  oauth_token_ciphertext  BYTEA,                         -- GitHub/GitLab OAuth token
  api_token_ciphertext    BYTEA,                         -- Jira/Linear/Zentao API token
  webhook_secret_ciphertext BYTEA,                       -- 接收侧验签
  event_mapping           JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{
    "finding_created":   "create_issue",
    "finding_confirmed": "create_issue",
    "scan_failed":       "create_issue_with_label_bug",
    "poc_proven":        "create_issue_with_label_security"
  }'::jsonb,
  default_labels          TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',   -- 推工单时自动打的标签
  default_assignees       TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',   -- 推工单时默认指派
  sync_direction          ticket_sync_direction_enum NOT NULL DEFAULT 'outbound_only',
                                                            -- outbound_only|inbound_outbound|bi_directional
  sync_state              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,  -- 是否启用双向同步
  enabled                 BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  last_sync_at            TIMESTAMPTZ,
  last_sync_status        sync_status_enum,
  last_sync_error         TEXT,
  created_by              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(id),
  created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  deleted_at              TIMESTAMPTZ
);
CREATE INDEX idx_ticket_integrations__customer ON integration.ticket_integrations(customer_id)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND enabled;
CREATE INDEX idx_ticket_integrations__tenant ON integration.ticket_integrations(tenant_id)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
-- 一个客户+系统组合只能有一个 active 集成
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_ticket_integrations__customer_system
  ON integration.ticket_integrations(customer_id, system)
  WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
COMMENT ON TABLE integration.ticket_integrations IS '工单系统集成(GitHub Issues/GitLab/Jira/Linear/Zentao);凭据 KMS 加密;事件映射驱动自动推单';
```

**RLS**:

```sql
ALTER TABLE integration.ticket_integrations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY ticket_int_tenant ON integration.ticket_integrations
  FOR ALL USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid);
CREATE POLICY ticket_int_customer ON integration.ticket_integrations
  FOR ALL USING (
    tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant', true)::uuid
    AND (
      customer_id = current_setting('app.current_customer', true)::uuid
        AND current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) = 'CustomerAdmin'
      OR current_setting('app.current_user_role', true) IN (
        'ProviderOwner','ProviderEngineer','ProviderAccountMgr','SystemBot'
      )
    )
  );
```

**与 `governance.webhooks` 关系**: `webhooks` 保留,用于**纯出站 webhook 推送**(无 OAuth 流程);`ticket_integrations` 用于**有状态的双向同步**(创单/回写状态/同步评论)。两者互补不重叠。

**阶段 2 联动**: 找到新 finding → 查 `ticket_integrations.event_mapping` → 调对应 API 创单 → 把返回的 `ticket_url` 写回 `detection.findings.ticket_ref`。

#### 13.19.6 P2 设计澄清(非新表,文档说明)

##### C1. `provider_applications` — 服务商入驻申请表单数据

**§2.1 引用**: 服务商首次接入"申请 + 人工审核"模式,表单字段(公司名 / 联系人 / 邮箱 / 手机 / 服务规模 / 客户量级)。

**设计决策(建议)**: **不新建表**,直接落 `core.tenants` 的 JSONB 扩展字段。理由:
- `tenants.status='pending'` 阶段的 tenant 行本身即"申请单"
- `core.tenants.white_label JSONB` 已有先例(JSONB 存非核心字段)
- 阶段 1 不需要"申请历史回溯"功能(只关心"通过/驳回"结果)

**ALTER**:

```sql
ALTER TABLE core.tenants
  ADD COLUMN application_payload JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;
  -- 申请时:{company_name, contact_name, contact_email, contact_phone,
  --         service_scale:'small'|'medium'|'large', customer_count_band:'1-10'|'11-50'|'>50',
  --         description}
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.tenants.application_payload IS '服务商申请表单数据;仅 pending/rejected 状态有意义;active 后可清理';
```

**驳回原因**: 用 `governance.audit_logs` 的 `customer_created`/`customer_suspended` 事件扩展 `event_type='provider_rejected'`,before/after_state 存驳回原因。

---

##### C2/C3. `governance.dashboard_snapshots` / `usage.usage_daily_summary`

**§13.14.1 引用**: 工作台 KPI 物化、趋势图按 capability 预聚合。

**设计决策**: **不建表,采用 materialized view + 60s Redis 缓存**。理由:
- 数据源 = `usage_events` / `findings` 实时聚合,无独立生命周期
- 历史快照价值低(用户看的是"现在")
- 物化视图 + 定期 REFRESH 即可满足 §13.14 性能目标

**DDL 草案**(放在迁移文件 `0011_materialized_views.sql`):

```sql
-- 工作台 KPI(每 60s REFRESH)
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW governance.dashboard_snapshots AS
SELECT
  tenant_id,
  customer_id,
  NOW() AS snapshot_at,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM detection.findings
     WHERE tenant_id = s.tenant_id AND customer_id IS NULL
       AND status IN ('open','in_progress') AND severity IN ('critical','high')
  ) AS open_critical_findings,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM detection.scan_runs
     WHERE tenant_id = s.tenant_id AND status IN ('queued','running')
  ) AS active_scan_runs,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM poc.poc_runs
     WHERE tenant_id = s.tenant_id AND status = 'pending'
  ) AS pending_poc_reviews
FROM (SELECT DISTINCT tenant_id, customer_id FROM usage.usage_events WHERE customer_id IS NOT NULL) s;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON governance.dashboard_snapshots(tenant_id, customer_id);
-- 配合 REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY(需 unique index)cron

-- 用量日聚合(每 1h REFRESH)
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW usage.usage_daily_summary AS
SELECT
  tenant_id, customer_id, project_id, capability, provider, model,
  DATE_TRUNC('day', occurred_at) AS day,
  sum(prompt_tokens) AS prompt_tokens,
  sum(completion_tokens) AS completion_tokens,
  sum(total_tokens) AS total_tokens,
  sum(cost_usd) AS cost_usd,
  count(*) AS event_count
FROM usage.usage_events
GROUP BY 1,2,3,4,5,6,7;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON usage.usage_daily_summary(tenant_id, customer_id, project_id, capability, provider, model, day);
```

---

#### 13.19.7 §13.18.12 速查表 + §13.18.16 审计增补

##### 速查表增补(§13.18.12)

| 表 | RLS 策略 | 主要索引 | 分区 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `core.contacts` | tenant + customer | customer_id, (tenant_id,email) partial, primary 唯一 | — |
| `core.password_reset_tokens` | tenant + SystemBot-only | user_id partial active, expires_at 清理 | — |
| `billing.plans` | 平台级(ProviderOwner + public_visible) | status+is_public+order | — |
| `core.users.notification_prefs` (ALTER) | 跟随 users 表 RLS | GIN jsonb_path_ops | — |
| `integration.ticket_integrations` | tenant + customer | customer_id partial, (customer,system) 唯一 | — |
| `governance.dashboard_snapshots` (MATVIEW) | 无(materialized view) | (tenant,customer) 唯一 | — |
| `usage.usage_daily_summary` (MATVIEW) | 无(materialized view) | (tenant,customer,project,cap,prov,model,day) 唯一 | — |

##### ON DELETE 修正(§13.18.16 增补)

| # | 表 | 字段 | 应改为 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 15 | `core.contacts` | `customer_id` | `... ON DELETE CASCADE` (已有) | 客户硬删时联系人跟随清 |
| 16 | `core.contacts` | `user_id` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 用户硬删时联系人保留(可能再激活) |
| 17 | `core.password_reset_tokens` | `user_id` | `... ON DELETE CASCADE` (已有) | 硬删用户时 token 必跟随清(隐私) |
| 18 | `billing.billing_accounts` | `plan` | `... REFERENCES billing.plans(code)` (新增 FK) | 套餐升级为 CRUD 后加 FK |
| 19 | `core.tenants` | `plan` | `... REFERENCES billing.plans(code)` (新增 FK) | 同上 |
| 20 | `integration.ticket_integrations` | `customer_id` | `... ON DELETE CASCADE` (已有) | 客户硬删时集成清理 |
| 21 | `integration.ticket_integrations` | `created_by` | `... ON DELETE SET NULL` | 创建人可被清 |

##### 反范式豁免(与 §13.18.16 一致)
- `core.contacts.notification_preference JSONB` — 保留独立字段,因联系人非 portal 用户时无 user_id 可关联
- `core.password_reset_tokens` 申请 IP / UA — 短期保留 7 天后清,不留痕

#### 13.19.8 新增 Enums(并入 §13.15)

```sql
-- §13.19.1 contacts
CREATE TYPE contact_role_enum AS ENUM ('primary','technical','billing','security','legal','other');
-- §13.19.2 password_reset
CREATE TYPE password_reset_via_enum AS ENUM ('web_form','admin_force','self_service');
-- §13.19.3 plans
CREATE TYPE plan_code_enum AS ENUM ('starter','pro','enterprise','private');
CREATE TYPE plan_status_enum AS ENUM ('active','archived','draft');
CREATE TYPE billing_period_enum AS ENUM ('monthly','quarterly','yearly');
-- §13.19.5 ticket_integrations
CREATE TYPE ticket_system_enum AS ENUM (
  'github_issues','gitlab_issues','jira','linear','zentao','redmine','custom'
);
CREATE TYPE ticket_sync_direction_enum AS ENUM ('outbound_only','inbound_outbound','bi_directional');
```

**注意**:`plan_code_enum` 与 §13.15 的 `billing_plan_enum` 重复。建议**统一为 `plan_code_enum`**(包含 `private`),删除 `billing_plan_enum`;`tenant_plan_enum` 同理改为 `plan_code_enum` 引用。

迁移命令:
```sql
-- ALTER TYPE
ALTER TYPE billing_plan_enum RENAME TO billing_plan_enum_legacy;
CREATE TYPE billing_plan_enum AS ENUM ('starter','pro','enterprise','private');
-- 数据迁移:INSERT INTO billing.plans(code) SELECT DISTINCT unnest(enum_range(...));
-- 字段类型变更(每个引用字段)
ALTER TABLE billing.billing_accounts
  ALTER COLUMN plan TYPE billing_plan_enum USING plan::text::billing_plan_enum;
-- 类同 core.tenants.plan
```

| 4 个新 Enums | `db/migrations/0018_enums_v2.sql`(先于 0011-0015 执行) |
| Materialized view REFRESH cron | `ops/cron/refresh_views.yaml` |
| password_reset 清理 cron(7 天滚动) | `ops/cron/password_reset_cleanup.yaml` |

**与已有迁移顺序**(新增 0011-0018 在 0010_pgboss.sql 之后执行):
- 0001-0009: §13.6/§13.18 基础表 + RLS
- 0010: pgboss / graphile-worker
- 0011-0015: §13.19 补充表(P0/P1)
- 0016: 物化视图
- 0017-0018: 新 RLS + 新 Enums

## 附录 A. 决策记录(ADR)
| # | 决策 | 日期 | 决策者 | 理由 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 目标用户为 MSSP/安全服务商 | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 与竞品差异化,市场需求明确 |
| 2 | 双门户架构(服务商+客户) | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 权责清晰,服务商掌控,客户只读 |
| 3 | 计费模式:按 AI token 用量 | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 与核心能力对齐,扫描免费降低门槛 |
| 4 | PoC 验证:AI 辅助生成+沙箱执行 | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 护城河能力,证明可利用性 |
| 5 | CI/CD:主动拉取+插件门控 | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 覆盖主动被动,客户侧侵入低 |
| 6 | 部署:纯 SaaS 共享多租户 | 2026-06-24 | PM/用户 | 成本最优,迭代快 |
| 7 | 视觉:企业级浅色商务 | 2026-06-24 | PM/前端专家 | 符合 MSSP 卖给企业的场景 |
| 8 | IA:客户中心+卡片工作台 | 2026-06-24 | PM/前端专家 | 符合 MSSP 心智,运营效率高 |
| 9 | 组件库:Element Plus | 2026-06-25 | 前端 | B2B 表格/表单/树成熟、原生中文 i18n、CSS Vars 白标换肤(§12.1.1) |
| 10 | 页面规格:逐页八段式 + 废弃旧原型 | 2026-06-25 | PM/前端 | 统一可交付规格;旧原型散点设计不符新 IA(决策 c) |
| 11 | 主数据库:PostgreSQL 15+ + RLS 多租户隔离 | 2026-06-26 | 后端 | 原生 RLS 兜底、JSONB/分区成熟、pgroll 在线迁移、避开 MySQL 缺 RLS;详见 §13 |

## 附录 B. 术语表
| 术语 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| Tenant | 租户,单个服务商 |
| Customer | 客户,服务商服务的甲方 |
| Project | 项目,代码仓库+扫描配置的单元 |
| DFG | Data Flow Graph,数据流图,自研引擎核心 |
| PoC | Proof of Concept,漏洞可利用性证明 |
| Finding | 漏洞发现,扫描产出的结果 |
| Token(AI) | LLM 调用的计费单元 |
| Token(代码) | GitHub/GitLab 授权访问码 |

## 附录 C. 与现状差异矩阵
| 维度 | 现状(v2原型) | 新设计 | 变化量 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 用户定位 | 单个安全工程师 | MSSP 服务多客户 | 巨大 |
| 架构 | 单租户单门户 | 双门户共享多租户 | 巨大 |
| 计费 | 无 | AI token 用量计费 | 新 |
| 代码源 | GitHub(仅Mock) | GitHub/GitLab/上传 | 新增 |
| 扫描引擎 | 基础静态规则 | 通用集成(SEMGREP/TRIVY) + 自研DFG | 重大升级 |
| PoC | 无 | AI辅助生成+沙箱执行 | 全新 |
| CI/CD | 无 | 主动拉取+插件门控+PR阻断 | 全新 |
| 工单集成 | 无 | GitHub Issues/GitLab/Jira/自定义webhook | 新增 |
| 报告 | 基础 | 多类型/可白标/合规映射 | 升级 |
| 治理 | 基础 | 审计日志/RBAC/合规框架对齐 | 升级 |
| 视觉 | 深色技术风 | 浅色商务风 | 完全重写 |
| 页面规格 | 无(仅原型散点) | §12 逐页八段式规格 | 全新 |
